## Crops

![TomatoPlantPic](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/growagarden/images/4/47/TomatoPlantPic.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/268?cb=20250429061116)

## Overview

**Crops** are one of the main and most important [mechanics](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Mechanics) of the game, serving as the main source of income.

They are planted as seeds in a garden, which can be obtained through purchasing at [Sam's Seed Shop](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Seed_Shop), opening any [Seed Packs](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Packs) or [Chests](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Chests), through [Merchants](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Merchants), in [Events](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Events) etc.

These seeds then grow into crops of various appearances with different [crop types](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Crops_Type) and values. When fully grown, the crop can yield mature produces that can be harvested, or the crop itself can be harvested fully.

The harvested produces can be used for exchanging them into![Sheckle](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/growagarden/images/0/08/Sheckle.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/20?cb=20260224181807) **Sheckles** by selling them at [Steven's Sell Stuff Stand](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Sell_Stuff), or for other purposes, like in [events](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Events) and such.

## Types of Crops

Single-Harvest Crops are crops that can be harvested whole when fully grown. They do not grow back after harvest and require another seed to be planted. The [Favorite Tool](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Favorite_Tool) can help prevent accidental harvesting.

Multi-Harvest Crops grow produce when fully grown. Their produce can regrow repeatedly, while the crop itself remains planted.

- [Single-Harvest](#)
- [Multi-Harvest](#)

## Trivia

*This section is a [trivia section](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Trivia_sections). Please [relocate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Handling_trivia#Recommendations_for_handling_trivia) any relevant information into other sections of the article.*

- The crop with the lowest chance of obtention was the [Venus Fly Trap](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Venus_Fly_Trap), at a 0.01% chance from the [Basic Seed Pack](https://growagarden.fandom.com/wiki/Basic_Seed_Pack).
